2026-10-23T04:14:19.1341901Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:aspire2ap] [build-only] Build the bounded SM90a verify companion (sources under kernels/) and retire rejected Decode probes

Rebuild of 502593f, whose run 39898959110 stopped in validate before the image build. The repository forbids trailing backslash line continuations under scripts, platforms, containers, aws and .github/workflows, and the CUDA_CHECK macro in fixed-specializations.patch has them. The companion sources therefore move from containers/fa3-verify to kernels/fa3-verify, following kernels/prefill/moe_routing.cu. build-sglang-container.sh still copies them to containers/fa3-verify in the build context, so the Dockerfile COPY and /opt/apac/fa3-verify do not change.

Why
Decode TARGET_VERIFY spends about 4.9 ms per 94-layer step in FA3. The current page64/N96 one-MMA configuration uses non-TMA loads, and batches above 33 requests acquire a long second persistent-scheduler wave. Build a small opt-in companion so one later serving allocation can screen the bounded alternatives without replacing the installed FlashAttention wheel. Keep the Decode cap at 128 and retain every request, query, KV token, FP8 input and BF16 final output.

What
Add SHA-verified pinned FA3 and CUTLASS sources, private symbols, fixed SM90a instantiations for control96, t64s2, t64s3, p128s2 and the shared split attention specialization, plus dedicated FP32 S2/S3 combines. Record source/compiler/flags/resource/binary provenance under opt/apac. Limit heavy compile concurrency to two and preserve the existing flash_ops binary.
Connect a TARGET_VERIFY-only overlay with a separate geometry-correct scheduler state, one prepare per step, capture-time cache clearing and 94 sequential layer calls. An empty setting keeps the existing B4 FA3 dispatch. Shared scratch buffers, explicit mutation schemas and changed-input graph checks cover the new path.
Replace the rejected router-gate and MegaMoE L2 probes with a four-GPU FA3 selector bounded to 180 seconds. Compare identical four-set KV inputs and complete 94-layer graphs in two alternating blocks. Only b32 may select a worker setting, requiring at least 3.2 microseconds per layer on all GPUs and blocks; b33 through b36 are diagnostics. Candidate exceptions are recorded and excluded without abandoning the other candidates. Keep the prior dense selection schedule, remove its FlashInfer candidate and isolate preparation/timing exceptions there too.
Remove the rejected gate/L2 overlays, host patch series entry, probe modes and associated tests. Add CPU selection/failure/metadata/loading coverage and GPU checks against the existing FA3 and an independent FP32 reference, including short lengths, empty splits and changed-input graph replays. GPU checks in serving select only configured kernels; exploratory screening stays in the selector.

Checks
CPU-only clean compilation inside the pinned Firmus image completed in 191 seconds including source retrieval, verification, linking and operator registration. The library is 2,393,176 bytes. All attention specializations report 255 static registers and zero stack or spill bytes. SASS confirms T64 KV TMA, P128 cp.async, FP8 WGMMA, BF16 final conversion and the FP32 partial/combine path. Existing flash_ops SHA256 is unchanged. The complete serving overlay chain applies to source extracted from the current image. The full CPU test suite passed: 584 passed and 45 existing conditional skips, with no failures. Ruff, Python/overlay syntax, shell syntax, patch application and build-tag checks passed. See the accompanying compile report and validation log for details.

Limits and rollout
No GPU job, Actions run, commit or push was performed while preparing this change. Numerical accuracy, changed-input GPU replay, the three-minute selector budget and performance remain unmeasured. A later serving job may select only candidates that pass the numeric and paired timing gates; otherwise it keeps the current FA3. Do not enable cluster/DSMEM, two-CTA occupancy changes or cap144, and do not infer an official goodput improvement from CPU compilation.
